# Bug ledger (week plan item 1: 1,000 real bugs)

Each entry is a bug a player could hit. It was reproduced before the fix (a host check or a measurement), proven gone after, and
fixed at its root. Item 5 (bloom) applies to every entry: after each fix, look for the same mistake elsewhere and log what turned
up. Newest at the bottom.

Count: 188

| # | Found | Bug | Root cause and fix | Proof | Bloom |

The bigger one: the sound bank's float copies of sounds made ahead of use (each new place, fight or vehicle warms a group) that were never heard, so never handed to the engine; the bank now counts its bytes and lets the oldest go past half the budget, never the common sounds (SoundBank.trim). | `questcheck --memsim DIR --minutes 120` (new: `--realtime`, memory after the allocator hands back free pages, heard and made-ahead audio apart): before (build 240, flat out): audio 34 -> 133 MB, still climbing, with 0 engine clips let go (the bank's made-ahead copies were the growth); after (build 241): 34 -> 75 MB by minute 30, 79 MB at two hours (heard 49 of the 64 MB budget, made ahead 30 of 32, 117 unheard copies let go), peak resident 1945 -> 1882 MB; in real time (`--realtime`, build 241) 35 -> 86 MB, peak 2000 -> 1796 MB | Other caches that only grow: chip damage (capped 4096), muzzles (600), wear marks (capped), fine prefetch (64): bounded. Three first tries were replaced: a link cleared in the deinit with job gates counted by thread (build 241's suite crashed as a world went with jobs in flight), the runtime's swift_tryRetain (reserved: it returned false for live objects, 80 checks failed on 243), and a lock round each read (244: passed, but 3% of the worst scene). IVec3 hashes with a fixed mixer as ChunkKey does (BS_OLDCELLHASH=1 for before). | BS_RCPROBE=1 (new, in the soak): on build 240 the world, the game and the ship manager slow, 8 of 31 ships; on 245 none of them, nor any of 38 ships. The ground's vertices beside a built block are pinned to its corners so the two meet; beside these they were not (natural blocks never pin), so the ground rounded away from the cube's vertical edges and left a wedge open. Such a block now pins the ground like any built block (SmoothTerrain.smoothQuads). `BS_OLDBUILTPIN=1` for before | Trailer harness (the store branch's `--cine` on a scratch build, town_street, QUEST_VOIDPROBE=1 QUEST_SKIP=sky,landmarks), build 345: frames 90-160 round the well, 6,367 magenta pixels before, 0 after | Plains and taiga villages lay cobblestone foundations (built: pinned already); savanna, desert and snowy ones lay natural blocks. `--scene orbit --kinds village` (8 shots round the nearest village, build 346): savanna villages 42 and 5 holes -> 0, a snowy one 3 -> 0; outposts and temples near them unchanged; five more trailer shots (town crane, capital, finale identical; the snow base's battle moves differently each run). Not this: the snow base gate's striped band under the deck (trailer item 5) |

## Check fixes (not counted: bugs in the host checks, not in the game)

| # | Found | Bug | Root cause and fix | Proof |
|---|-------|-----|--------------------|-------|
| 1 | Nov 3 | FactionRaidsTests expected two dropships, but a raid lands one | The test was out of date. | the raids check |
| 2 | Nov 4 | "balance gear pays" failed at random (Capital Trooper one run, a Gravesworn another) | A trooper's vest takes a blow by where it lands, so one duel's kill time was luck: 4.6 s early and 5.3 s iron on one run, 3.3 s iron on the next. The duel against troopers now takes the middle of seven seeds. | the --render suite |
| 3 | Nov 4 | The encounter check's corsair sloop never came in | Check bug: the raid part at his home left SeaHunt.ashore over a minute, and the hunt's first tick sent the sloop away before a second at sea reset it. In play the count is 0 by the time a hunter comes. | --encounters: the sloop removed at frame 0 before |
| 4 | Nov 4 | The friendly-fire check's Meridian squad never fought | Check bug: an earlier group of checks left the factions' standing changed (the Meridian friendly to him), so the squad had no one to fight. The check now starts from an empty standing and puts it back, and every check group's wrapper (`leaves`) now snapshots the standing and the next hunters, puts them back and says when a group changed them (build 126: greywall, balance and p12 did). | the --render suite, build 126 |
| 5 | Nov 4 | The frame soak's frigate scene measured an empty sky | Check bug: it spawned role "frigate", which no ship has (the Stormwarden is "warfrigate"), so it timed nothing. | --framesoak --only frigate |
| 6 | Nov 4 | "P510 links" (--rail-only) failed on every build since 62: 9 of 128 lines link, and it asked for a third | The target was set before P511. Links seldom fit (the next line along the row is across the sea or past towns, up to three stretches on), so P511 made an end with no link turn in a loop, and the P511 loops check counts what he sees: 239 of 256 line ends run on by a link or a loop, 17 keep buffers. The links check now asks that some lines link and that every link joins its lines end to end. | --rail-only on build 142: every other rail check passes; P511 loops 239 of 256 |
| 7 | Nov 4 | Five checks of the Mac's mob suite failed on their first run on the headset build | Each was older than a change it didn't know: strays left the spawn lists with the skeleton bowmen (P246/P483); retired guns are no items; an alert is a spoken line where one fits (P468), not the bark; a trooper shoots through glass (P305 b) but holds at a stone wall (P419 c), which the check now tries; a citadel's mobs carry a role after their kind ("soldier_ironclad:heavy"). | questcheck --mobtests, build 145 |
| 8 | Nov 4 | Three checks of the VR melee suite (`--swingtest`, first run on the headset build) failed | Older than the changes they met: P483 made a sword in creative break nothing, so "the sword mines once the mob is gone" could never pass in creative (the mining checks now run in survival); R11 made a swing's damage its blade's speed, so a quick re-swing is as hard (it asked it to be weak). In survival the test's platform 60 up in the air is no ground in a smooth world (the field is air there), so he mines at the airborne fifth: the checks give it 5 s and say so. | --swingtest: 3 failed on build 146, 0 on build 149 |
| 9 | Nov 4 | The V78 cave spawn check failed one run in two (0, 1 or 1 monsters within 48 blocks of him) | Older than H1 (his ask that cave monsters spawn about 10 blocks farther off, the nearest 34), which left a 34-48 ring. It now counts within 64. A change drawing cave samples nearer him didn't help (1, 4, 1) and was taken out. | --v78, build 150 |
| 10 | Nov 4 | "Spawn monsters come in packs" failed once (build 149) with 9 zombies out | It bucketed monsters into 12-block squares, so a pack across an edge read as lone ones. It now counts monsters with one of their kind within 10 blocks. | --mobtests, build 151 |
| 11 | Nov 4 | The V78 cave check still failed about one run in five (1 monster within 64 blocks on build 150, asking for 2) | A run sees about three cave spawns near him, so 2 of them by chance is a thin ask. Three runs are counted together (want 4 within 64 and 9 under him). | --v78 on build 154: 6 within 64, 58 under |
| 12 | Nov 4 | Three golem summoning checks failed on build 154 | Each summon tries 10 random spots, and on the test spawn's broken ground all 10 can miss; one call was asked to summon. The checks give it five calls (in play each villager calls it every 5 s while gossiping). | --mobtests on build 155: 0 failed |
| 13 | Nov 5 | The copper suite's arrows never pressed a button or a wooden plate (2 of 111 failed) | Check bug: the headset harness never ticked the game before its suites, so World.activeCenter stayed at the origin, thousands of blocks off, and nothing near the player ticked (P376). questcheck now sets it at load, as the game's tick does. | --coppertest: 109 of 111 on build 158, 111 of 111 on build 159 |
| 14 | Nov 5 | 22 checks of the Mac's PM9 list failed on their first run on the headset build (a 23rd was entry 22) | Older than what came after: G18 (Oct 11, "plain grass looks barren") brought grass back in patches with tall tufts, which has its own checks; P483 (Oct 28) put the surface density at 0.6, not 0.5; since Q2c-console-58 a refused bone meal is still "handled" (with a note), so the sugar cane check counts the bone meal; a handful of animal packs round the spawn can't show a ratio (the 14,400-chunk roll check does). | --questbugs: 31 failed on build 158, 9 on build 159 |
| 15 | Nov 5 | The cave spawn check (V78 list) still failed now and then: 1 and 3 monsters within 64 blocks in three runs | Its "a fifth land within 64 blocks" was never measured: cave monsters come up in every cave at every depth over 13 x 13 chunks (as in the reference), and with H1's 34-block minimum 6 of 58, 1 of 55 and 3 of 71 landed within 64 in three-run sets. A structure too big for the check near a change is now weighed whole (`StructureFlow`): each block's weight must find a way to the ground through joints that carry only so much (straight down the weaker block's crushing strength, half of it for a lone column; hung, sideways or along an edge a share of the bond). That is a flow of weight; where it cannot all get down, the full joints are where it breaks and everything above them comes down. In play (`StructureCollapse`): the piece is read a slice a frame under the tick's carving allowance, changes landing meanwhile are read into it (never a restart), the weighing runs on a worker thread, and what falls is taken out a piece a frame, lowest first, as rigid bodies of at most a 12-block cube that wait for the workers' redraw. Concrete and steel crush at a wall of themselves 300 high, not the real 1,250 (on the real numbers three posts carried his tower's top). Only what stood before and has no way down now comes down, in lots of 4 cells a side from the bottom up, a few a frame; the opened cells stay listed and the fallen join them, so what hung from it is weighed next against the ground as it first was, up to the surface. Ground with no way down even before (an overhang, an arch, an island the generator made) is left: the world never crumbles on its own. Strengths are the materials' own (Structure.Mat): earth, sand and gravel hold a room up to about 17 cells across, rock holds any span a carve makes and gives way at a neck; a built block under ground carries it, water does not; ground nothing breaks holds. BS_NOBRIDGEHUNT=1. (b) The weigh ran on every trestle round a ridden train and judged any deck cell 3 or more from a standing leg as hanging: a diagonal line's trestle (every field-world line between towns, every open-country line) had edge legs only at its staircase cells, so its straight runs stood on centre piers 8 apart. Fix: cached ground keys on groundStamp(x, z) = fieldStamp + a per-area stamp (16 x 16 columns, folded 64 x 64); craters bump only the areas within their reach + 3, arriving/refilled/settled chunks theirs + 3 (World.groundChanged); other field changes still bump the world stamp. The shape cache holds 16384 cells (2 MB). Framesoak prints "ground shapes rebuilt". Fix: the levels above were already checked on the way down, and a blocked level blocks every deeper drop, so each level reads only its one new cell and stops at the first blocked one.
